Pseudo:
def fw_optimize(n, p, c, m, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic(); t=0.02; K=1; stall=0; step=0
    best=p.copy(); best_m=m
    L=2*n-1; N = 1 << (max(1,3*n-2)-1).bit_length()? For periodic FFT refresh.
    min_t=1e-5
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        # recompute M from c (c may drift)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_m - 1e-15: best=p.copy(); best_m=M
        if stall > 50:
             # perturb
             ...
             c = convolve_normalized(p,N) ; M=c.max(); reset
        KtoUse = 1
        if stall > 10 or step%100<5: KtoUse=3?
        j = choose_j(c,p,n,KtoUse,rng)
        # If j same for many? maybe random low.
        shifted = np.zeros(L); shifted[j:j+n] = p
        dj=2*j
        improved=False
        # Try current t, maybe double/triple if works
        trial_t = min(0.2, max(min_t, 2.0/(step+2.0)?))
        # Let's set base_t = current_t.
        # Evaluate candidate function inline maybe.
        best_t=0; best_trial_M=M
        for factor in [1.0, 2.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]:
            tt = current_t*factor
            if tt<=0 or tt>1: continue
            a2=(1-tt)*(1-tt); b=2*tt*(1-tt)
            # Instead of full vals=max, we can compute M_trial = max(a2*c + b*shifted + tt^2 at dj)
            vals = a2*c + b*shifted
            val_dj = vals[dj] + tt*tt
            M_trial = max(np.max(vals), val_dj)
            if M_trial < best_trial_M:
                best_trial_M=M_trial; best_t=tt
                if M_trial < M: improved=True
            # if improved and factor=2 maybe continue? okay.
        if improved and best_t>0:
             tt=best_t
             a2=...; b=...
             c = a2*c + b*shifted
             if 2*tt? use tt*tt; c[2*j] += tt*tt
             p *= (1-tt); p[j] += tt
             s=p.sum(); if abs(s-1)>1e-10: p/=s; c? scaling? If p scaled by 1/s, c should scale by 1/s^2; to avoid c mismatch, just recompute c via FFT if s drift >1e-8.
             M = c.max()
             if M < best_m: best=p.copy(); best_m=M
             stall=0; step+=1
             # adapt current_t
             current_t = min(0.1, max(min_t, current_t*1.05 if factor?))
        else:
             current_t=max(min_t, current_t*0.5)
             stall+=1
             # If t very small, try random j?
        if step % 50 == 0: refresh c via FFT? But stepping exact; occasional.
           p /= p.sum()
           c = convolve_norm(p,N)
           M=c.max()
           if M<best_m: best=p.copy(); best_m=M
    return best,best_m

Perturbation:
def perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    n=len(p); p = p.copy()
    # several blocks
    for _ in range(3): start... p[start:stop]*=exp(rng.normal(0,sigma));
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-15, None); p/=p.sum(); return p
sigma maybe 0.05/0.2.

def choose_j(c,p,n,K,rng,noise=0.0):

       # Use argpartition. If K small and n large.
       idx = np.argpartition(c, len(c)-K)[-K:]
       g = np.zeros(n)
       # maybe weights c-based? Equal. Could weight by c values to emphasize max: w = c[idx]/sum(c[idx]); but equal fine.
       for k in idx:
          l = k-n+1
          if l <0: l=0
          r = k

          # p_slice_start = k-r, p_slice_end = k-l
          # We need add p[p_slice_end : p_slice_start-1 : -1]

Now perturb function top-level:
def perturb(p, rng, sigma):
   n=len(p); q=np.array(p, dtype=float, copy=True)
   q = np.maximum(q, 1e-15)
   for _ in range(3):
      width = max(1, n//100)
      start = int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
      q[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
      if rng.random()<0.5:
         start2=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1)); q[start2:start2+width]*=exp(rng.normal(0,sigma*2))
   q = np.clip(q,1e-15,1.0); s=q.sum(); if s>0 q/=s else q.fill(1/n)
   return q
This can create p_j up to maybe large but line search okay. It cannot represent exact zeros; floor 1e-15.

But if p has small values 1e-15 and many, sum floor n*1e-15 negligible. Good.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def normalize_vec(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
    s = v.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       p = np.full(v.size, 1.0/v.size)
       return p
    return v/s

def convolve_norm(p, N=None):
    n = p.size
    L = 2*n - 1
    if N is None:
       N = next_pow2(L)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F*F.conjugate(), N)[:L]
    # remove tiny negatives
    c[ c < 0 ] = ? cannot vector mask assignment efficient? c[np.minimum?]. Use c = np.where(c < 0, 0, c) maybe.
    return c
```
But setting all negative to0 could hide errors; maybe use np.clip(c,0,None). This may make sum c slightly > but okay for search. For exact score? It might artificially lower max if negative? Negative values not max. Fine.

```
def quick_score(p):
    n=p.size; c=convolve_norm(p); return 2*n*c.max(), c
```

make_initials:
```
def make_initials(n, rng):
    idx=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x=idx/(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
    base=1.0/np.sqrt(idx+1.0)
    rev=base[::-1].copy()
    cands=[base.copy(), rev.copy(), np.ones(n)]
    # parent? handled outside.
    for beta in (-2.0,-1.0,0.0,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       cands.append(base * np.exp(beta*x))
    for beta in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
       cands.append(np.exp(beta*x))
       cands.append(np.exp(-beta*x))
    for lam in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
       cands.append(base * (1.0 + lam*x))
       cands.append(base * (1.0 + lam*(1.0-x)))
    for alpha in (0.01,0.05,0.2,1.0):
       cands.append(base + alpha)
    for alpha in (0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0):
       cands.append(base + alpha*rev)
    for beta in (1.0,2.0):
       for alpha in (0.1,0.3,1.0):
           e=np.exp(beta*x)
           cands.append(base + alpha*e)
           if beta>1: cands.append(base + alpha*np.exp(-beta*x))
    # endpoint additions raw
    for ep in (0.2,1.0,3.0):
       a=base.copy(); a[0]+=ep; cands.append(a)
       a=base.copy(); a[-1]+=ep; cands.append(a)
       a=base.copy(); a[0]+=ep; a[-1]+=ep; cands.append(a)
    # random
    for _ in range(3):
       cands.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,size=n)))
    # filter finite positive
    uniq=[]
    for a in cands:
       a=np.asarray(a,float)
       if a.size==n and np.isfinite(a).all() and a.min()>=0:
          uniq.append(a)
    # avoid too many >50
    if len(uniq)>50: uniq=uniq[:50]
    return uniq
```
Potential endpoint raw addition ep=3 to base first value 1 makes 4; okay. For base*exp positive.

def score_initials(n, raw_list, rng):
    scored=[]
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    for raw in raw_list:
       p=normalize_vec(raw)
       if p.size==n:
          c=convolve_norm(p,N)
          M=float(c.max())
          scored.append((M,p,c))

Optimize function choose_pair:
```
def choose_pair(c,p,n,K,rng):
    L=c.size
    if K >= L: K=L
    if K == 0: K=1
    # top active
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    # weights
    w = c[idx]
    wsum = w.sum()
    if wsum <=0: w = np.ones_like(idx,dtype=float)
    else: w = w/wsum
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
       k=int(kk)
       l = k - n + 1
       if l <0: l=0
       r = k
       if r >= n: r=n-1
       start = k - r
       end = k - l
       # add ww * p reversed slice
       g[l:r+1] += ww * p[end:start-1:-1]
    # deterministic pair
    j = int(np.argmin(g))
    d = int(np.argmax(g))
    if d == j or p[d] <= 1e-12:
       # use argpartition for candidates
       if n > 10:
          min_idx = np.argpartition(g, 10)[:10]
          max_idx = np.argpartition(g, n-10)[-10:]
       else:
          min_idx = np.argsort(g)[:min(10,n)]
          max_idx = np.argsort(g)[::-1][:min(10,n)]
       # deterministic through list with rng shuffle? no lambda
       for jj in min_idx:
          for dd in max_idx:
             if jj != dd and p[dd] > 1e-12:
                j=int(jj); d=int(dd); break
          if j != int(np.argmin(g)) or d != int(np.argmax(g)):
             break
    return j,d
```
But the nested break logic flawed. Simpler in optimize: call choose_pair returns j,d; if p[d] tiny, call choose_pair_random. Implement separate:
```
def choose_pair_attacked(c,p,n,K,rng,randomize=False):
   ... compute g, return g? To avoid recompute for random, return g.
```

def compute_gradient(c,p,n,K, g):
   idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w = c[idx]; s=w.sum(); if s>0: w=w/s; else w fill1
   g.fill(0)
   for ...: g[l:r+1] += w[pairindex] * p[end:start-1:-1]

def choose_from_gradient(g,p,rng):
   # deterministic if not random
   if not randomize:
      j=int(np.argmin(g)); d=int(np.argmax(g))
      if p[d] > 1e-12 and j != d: return j,d
   # random among top 12
   m = min(12, g.size)
   min_idx = np.argpartition(g, m)[:m]
   max_idx = np.argpartition(g, g.size-m)[-m:]
   for _ in range(50):
      a=int(min_idx[int(rng.integers(m))]); b=int(max_idx[int(rng.integers(m))])
      if a != b and p[b] > 1e-12: return a,b
   # fallback scan
   for dd in range(g.size-1,-1,-1): if p[dd]>1e-12 and dd!=j: return j,dd
   for jj in range(g.size):
      for dd in range(g.size-1,-1,-1): ...
   return 0,0

```
def evaluate_exchange_delta(c, diff, j, d, temp, delta):
    np.copyto(temp, c)
    factor = 2.0*delta
    temp += factor * diff
    d2 = delta*delta
    two_j=2*j; two_d=2*d; jd=j+d
    temp[two_j] += d2
    temp[two_d] += d2
    temp[jd] -= 2.0*d2
    return float(temp.max())
```

Optimize_exchange pseudo full:

def optimize_exchange(n, p, c, M, time_limit, rng):
    start=time.monotonic()
    p=p.astype(float,copy=True); c=c.astype(float,copy=True)
    L=2*n-1
    best=p.copy(); best_m=M
    g=np.zeros(n); diff=np.zeros(L); temp=np.empty(L)
    current_fr? For exchange fraction fixed list; no adapt.
    stall=0; steps=0
    min_frac=0.002
    frac_primary=(1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1)
    frac_secondary=(0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005)
    while time.monotonic()-start < time_limit:
        # periodic refresh
        if steps % 200 ==0 and steps>0:
            p /= p.sum(); c=convolve_norm(p); M=float(c.max()); if M<best_m: best_m=M; best=p.copy()
        M=float(c.max())
        if M < best_m:
            best_m=M; best=p.copy()
        Kuse=3
        if stall > 20: Kuse=5
        if stall > 60: Kuse=8
        compute_gradient(c,p,n,Kuse,g)
        randomize = (stall > 5) or (rng.random()<0.07)
        j,d=choose_from_gradient(g,p,rng,randomize)
        pd=p[d]
        if pd <= 1e-12:
            stall +=1
            # try to move any positive max? maybe random
            if stall % 20 ==0: perturb...
            continue
        diff.fill(0.0)
        diff[j:j+n] += p
        diff[d:d+n] -= p
        improved=False
        best_fr=0.0; best_trial=M
        for fr in frac_primary:
            delta=fr*pd
            if delta <= 1e-15: continue
            Mtr=evaluate_exchange_delta(c,diff,j,d,temp,delta)
            if Mtr < best_trial:
                best_trial=Mtr; best_fr=fr
            if Mtr < M:
                improved=True
                # we can still check smaller? maybe if fr>0.25, continue to find smaller? We'll break after primary? We want best among primary; continue through all primary to get smallest if better.
        if not improved:
            for fr in frac_secondary:
                delta=fr*pd
                if delta <= 1e-15: continue
                Mtr=evaluate...
                if Mtr < best_trial:
                    best_trial=Mtr; best_fr=fr
                if Mtr < M:
                    improved=True
        if improved and best_fr>0:
            delta=best_fr*pd
            factor=2.0*delta; d2=delta*delta
            c += factor*diff
            c[2*j] += d2; c[2*d] += d2; c[j+d] -= 2.0*d2
            p[j] += delta; p[d] -= delta
            if p[d] < 0.0: p[d]=0.0
            # normalize tiny sum? sum should 1; if drift
            ss=p.sum()
            if abs(ss-1.0)>1e-9:
                p /= ss; c /= (ss*ss)
            # clip small negative in c?
            # if many negative, refresh
            if np.any(c < -1e-10):
                p /= p.sum(); c=convolve_norm(p); M=float(c.max())
            else:
                M=float(c.max())
            steps +=1; stall=0
            if M < best_m:
                best_m=M; best=p.copy()
        else:
            stall +=1
            if stall % 25 ==0:
                # perturb
                p0=p.copy(); c0=c.copy()
                sig=0.02 + 0.04*min(3, stall//50)
                p=perturb(p,rng,sig)
                c=convolve_norm(p)
                M=float(c.max())
                if M > max(best_m*1.2, best_m+1e-8):
                    p=p0; c=c0
                else:
                    # accept perturbed state
                    # Do not update best unless better
                    pass
                stall=0
    return best,best_m
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline = start + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    # parent
    global_n = None
    try:
       par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if par.size >=16 and par.size <= 20000:
           p=normalize_vec(par); c=convolve_norm(p); M=float(c.max()); score=2*par.size*M
           if score<best_score: best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    except Exception:
       pass
    # predefined runs: tuple (n, space) maybe.
    # Since budget 1800, use dynamic:
    n_choices=[6121, 8192, 4096, 2048]
    # We will process in order. For each, time limit based on remaining and importance.
    n_budget = {6121:700, 8192:700, 4096:250, 2048:150}
    for n in n_choices:
       remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if remaining < 90: break
       allo = min(n_budget.get(n,150), remaining-5)
       if allo < 60: continue
       raw=make_initials(n,rng)
       # include parent if length matches n? maybe not.
       topk = 3 if n >= 4096 else 4
       top_list = score_initials(n, raw, topk)
       # If the top candidate's potential lower than global best? Not known.
       # split time among top candidates: first 50%, second 30%, third 20%
       sub_budgets = []
       t=allo
       for i in range(len(top_list)):
           if i==0: b=t*0.55
           elif i==1: b=t*0.30
           else: b=t*0.15
           b=min(b, remaining-5)
           if b < 20: continue
           M,p,c = top_list[i]
           remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
           actual=min(b, remaining-5)
           if actual < 15: break
           bp,bm=optimize_exchange(n,p,c,M,actual,rng)
           sc=2*n*bm
           if sc < best_score:
              best_score=sc; best_p=bp.copy()
              print(f"new best score {sc:.6f} n={n}")
           t -= actual
    # final polish on global best
    remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if best_p is not None and remaining > 120:
       n=len(best_p)
       p=best_p.copy()
       c=convolve_norm(p); M=float(c.max())
       bp,bm=optimize_exchange(n,p,c,M,min(remaining-5, 600), rng)
       sc=2*n*bm
       if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=bp.copy()
    # final exact eval of best and a few fallback
    if best_p is None: best_p=np.ones(256)
    # Verify with evaluate_sequence; if inf, try uniform.
    try:
       exact = float(evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist()))
       if np.isfinite(exact): best_score=exact
    except Exception: best_score=np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
       # fallback: use a simple normalized singular length 1024
       n=1024; a=1/np.sqrt(np.arange(n)+1); best_p=normalize_vec(a); exact=evaluate_sequence(best_p.tolist())
    return [float(x) for x in best_p]
```

def choose_from_gradient(g, p, rng, randomize):
    n = g.size
    if n == 1: return 0,0
    if not randomize:
        j = int(np.argmin(g)); d = int(np.argmax(g))
        if dd? if p[d] > 1e-12 and d != j: return j,d
    m = min(12, n)
    min_idx = np.argpartition(g, m-1)[:m] if m < n else np.arange(n)
    max_idx = np.argpartition(g, n-m)[-m:] if m < n else np.arange(n)[::-1]
    for _ in range(100):
        jj = int(min_idx[int(rng.integers(m))])
        dd = int(max_idx[int(rng.integers(m))])
        if jj != dd and p[dd] > 1e-12:
            return jj, dd
    # fallback deterministic
    for dd in range(n-1, -1, -1):
        if p[dd] > 1e-12:
            j = int(np.argmin(g))
            if j != dd: return j,dd
            # choose second min
            for jj in range(n):
                if jj != dd and p[jj] >=0:
                    return jj,dd
    return 0,n-1
```

Compute_gradient:
```
def compute_gradient(c,p,n,K,g):
    L=c.size
    if K <=0: K=1
    if K >= L: K=L
    if K == L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, L-K)[-K:]  # selecting last K
    w=c[idx]
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws <=0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
       w=np.ones_like(w, dtype=float)
    else:
       w=w/ws
    g.fill(0.0)
    for t in range(K):
       k=int(idx[t]); ww=float(w[t])
       if ww ==0: continue
       l = k - n + 1
       if l < 0: l = 0
       r = k
       if r >= n: r = n-1
       start = k - r
       end = k - l
       # p slice length r-l+1 = end-start+1
       g[l:r+1] += ww * p[end:start-1:-1]
```
If n=1, L=1, k=0, l/r0, start/end0, slice p[0:-1:-1], okay.

Evaluate_exchange_delta:
```
def evaluate_exchange_delta(c, diff, j, d, temp, delta):
    np.copyto(temp, c)
    if delta ==0: return float(temp.max())
    temp += (2.0*delta) * diff
    d2=delta*delta
    two_j=2*j; two_d=2*d; jd=j+d
    temp[two_j] += d2
    temp[two_d] += d2
    temp[jd] -= 2.0*d2
    return float(temp.max())
```
If jd equals two_j? only if d=j; we avoid. If jd > L? j,d≤n-1, jd≤2n-2=L-1; okay.

```
def perturb_sequence(p, rng, sigma):
    n=p.size
    q=np.array(p, dtype=float, copy=True)
    q=np.where(q>1e-15, q, 1e-15)
    for _ in range(4):
       width = max(1, n//100)
       start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
       q[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
       if rng.random() < 0.5:
          width2=max(1,n//50)
          start2=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-width2+1)))
          q[start2:start2+width2] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*1.5)))
    q=np.clip(q,1e-15,1.0)
    s=q.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: q.fill(1.0/n)
    else: q/=s
    return q
```

def score_initials(n, raw_list, topk):
    N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    storage=[]; scores=[]
    for raw in raw_list:
       p=normalize_vec(raw)
       if p.size != n: continue
       c=convolve_norm(p,N)
       M=float(c.max())
       if np.isfinite(M):
          storage.append((p,c)); scores.append(M)
    if not scores: return []
    order=np.argsort(scores)
    out=[]
    for idx in order[:topk]:
       p,c=storage[int(idx)]
       out.append((float(scores[int(idx)]), p, c))
    return out
```

```python
"""
Local-search optimizer for nonnegative step heights minimizing
J(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

The solver works on normalized probabilities (sum = 1) and performs exact
coordinate exchanges using an O(n) update of the full convolution.  Each
acceptance step line-searches over transferring a fraction of one coordinate
to another, using the exact quadratic update of the convolution.  Initial
sequences are generated from several analytic shapes and random perturbations.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_vec(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
    s = v.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(v.size, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return v / s


def convolve_norm(p, N=None):
    """Exact convolution of normalized p with itself, via FFT."""
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None or N < L:
        N = next_pow2(L)
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F.conjugate(), N)[:L]
    # Tiny negative FFT roundoff is harmless for the max, but clean it.
    if c.size and c.min() < 0.0:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def perturb_sequence(p, rng, sigma):
    """Nonnegative multiplicative block perturbation, renormalized."""
    q = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    n = q.size
    q = np.where(q > 1e-15, q, 1e-15)
    for _ in range(4):
        width = max(1, n // 100)
        hi = max(1, n - width + 1)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, hi))
        q[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        if rng.random() < 0.55:
            width2 = max(1, n // 50)
            hi2 = max(1, n - width2 + 1)
            start2 = int(rng.integers(0, hi2))
            q[start2:start2 + width2] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.7 * sigma)))
    q = np.clip(q, 1e-15, 1.0)
    s = q.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        q.fill(1.0 / max(1, n))
    else:
        q /= s
    return q


def make_initials(n, rng):
    """Return a list of raw nonnegative candidate vectors of length n."""
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        x = idx / float(n - 1)
    else:
        x = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)

    base = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx + 1.0)
    rev = base[::-1].copy()

    out = [base.copy(), rev.copy(), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]

    # One-sided singular shape tilted in both directions.
    for beta in (-2.0, -1.0, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        out.append(base * np.exp(beta * x))

    # Pure exponentials.
    for beta in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        out.append(np.exp(beta * x))
        out.append(np.exp(-beta * x))

    # Smooth tilts of the singular shape.
    for lam in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        out.append(base * (1.0 + lam * x))
        out.append(base * (1.0 + lam * (1.0 - x)))

    # Uniform floor corrections.
    for alpha in (0.01, 0.05, 0.2, 1.0):
        out.append(base + alpha)

    # Add a weaker opposite singular (endpoint layer).
    for alpha in (0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0):
        out.append(base + alpha * rev)

    # Add smooth right/broad bumps.
    for beta in (1.0, 2.0):
        e = np.exp(beta * x)
        for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0):
            out.append(base + alpha * e)
        e2 = np.exp(-beta * x)
        for alpha in (0.1, 0.3, 1.0):
            out.append(base + alpha * e2)

    # Endpoint atom corrections.
    for ep in (0.2, 1.0, 3.0):
        a = base.copy(); a[0] += ep; out.append(a)
        a = base.copy(); a[-1] += ep; out.append(a)
        a = base.copy(); a[0] += ep; a[-1] += ep; out.append(a)

    # Some random positive shapes.
    for _ in range(4):
        out.append(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)))
        out.append(np.power(np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)), 0.3) + 1e-12)

    cleaned = []
    for a in out:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size != n:
            continue
        if not np.isfinite(a).all():
            continue
        if a.min() < 0.0:
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
        cleaned.append(a)

    if len(cleaned) > 60:
        cleaned = cleaned[:60]
    return cleaned


def score_initials(n, raw_list, topk):
    """Normalize and score raw candidates; return up to topk (M,p,c)."""
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    storage = []
    scores = []
    for raw in raw_list:
        p = normalize_vec(raw)
        if p.size != n:
            continue
        c = convolve_norm(p, N)
        M = float(c.max())
        if np.isfinite(M):
            storage.append((p, c))
            scores.append(M)
    if not storage:
        return []
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for idx in order[:max(1, topk)]:
        i = int(idx)
        p, c = storage[i]
        out.append((float(scores[i]), p, c))
    return out


def compute_gradient(c, p, n, K, g):
    """
    Weighted subgradient-like vector g for the current convolution c.
    For an active coefficient k, the derivative contribution to p_i is
    p_{k-i} (where valid).  We average several near-max coefficients.
    """
    L = c.size
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, L - K)[-K:]

    w = c[idx].astype(np.float64)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 1e-300:
        w = np.ones_like(w, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    g.fill(0.0)
    for t in range(len(idx)):
        k = int(idx[t])
        ww = float(w[t])
        if ww == 0.0:
            continue
        l = k - n + 1
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = k
        if r >= n:
            r = n - 1
        start = k - r
        end = k - l
        # i ranges over [l, r], and p index is k - i, decreasing.
        g[l:r + 1] += ww * p[end:start - 1:-1]


def choose_from_gradient(g, p, rng, randomize):
    """Choose donor d and recipient j for a positive transfer d -> j."""
    n = g.size
    if n == 1:
        return 0, 0

    if not randomize:
        j = int(np.argmin(g))
        d = int(np.argmax(g))
        if p[d] > 1e-12 and d != j:
            return j, d

    m = min(16, n)
    if m < n:
        min_idx = np.argpartition(g, m - 1)[:m]
        max_idx = np.argpartition(g, n - m)[-m:]
    else:
        min_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        max_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[::-1]

    for _ in range(80):
        jj = int(min_idx[int(rng.integers(m))])
        dd = int(max_idx[int(rng.integers(m))])
        if jj != dd and p[dd] > 1e-12:
            return jj, dd

    j = int(np.argmin(g))
    for dd in range(n - 1, -1, -1):
        if p[dd] > 1e-12 and dd != j:
            return j, dd
    for jj in range(n):
        if jj != j:
            return jj, int(np.argmax(g))
    return 0, max(1, n - 1)


def evaluate_exchange_delta(c, diff, j, d, temp, delta):
    """
    Exact trial max coefficient after transferring delta from d to j.
    diff = shift_j(p) - shift_d(p).
    """
    np.copyto(temp, c)
    if delta == 0.0:
        return float(temp.max())
    temp += (2.0 * delta) * diff
    d2 = delta * delta
    two_j = 2 * j
    two_d = 2 * d
    jd = j + d
    temp[two_j] += d2
    temp[two_d] += d2
    temp[jd] -= 2.0 * d2
    return float(temp.max())


def optimize_exchange(n, p, c, M, time_limit, rng):
    """
    Coordinate-exchange local search with exact O(n) convolution updates.
    Returns best p and best normalized max coefficient M.
    """
    if n < 2 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return p.copy(), float(M)

    p = np.array(p, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    c = np.array(c, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    best = p.copy()
    best_m = float(M)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    diff = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    temp = np.empty(L, dtype=np.float64)

    frac_primary = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1)
    frac_secondary = (0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005)
    stall = 0
    steps = 0
    start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - start < time_limit:
        # Periodic exact refresh to annihilate accumulated roundoff.
        if steps and steps % 150 == 0:
            p = normalize_vec(p)
            c = convolve_norm(p)
            M = float(c.max())
            if M < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = M
                best = p.copy()

        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            p = normalize_vec(p)
            c = convolve_norm(p)
            M = float(c.max())

        if M < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m = M
            best = p.copy()

        Kuse = 3
        if stall > 15:
            Kuse = 5
        if stall > 45:
            Kuse = 8

        compute_gradient(c, p, n, Kuse, g)
        randomize = (stall > 4) or (rng.random() < 0.08)
        j, d = choose_from_gradient(g, p, rng, randomize)
        pd = float(p[d])

        if pd <= 1e-13:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                p0 = p.copy()
                c0 = c.copy()
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.04 * min(3, stall // 50)
                pp = perturb_sequence(p, rng, sigma)
                cc = convolve_norm(pp)
                MM = float(cc.max())
                # Accept perturbation only if not catastrophically worse.
                if np.isfinite(MM) and MM <= max(best_m * 1.25, best_m + 1e-7):
                    p = pp
                    c = cc
                    M = MM
                else:
                    p = p0
                    c = c0
                    M = best_m
                stall = 0
            continue

        diff.fill(0.0)
        diff[j:j + n] += p
        diff[d:d + n] -= p

        improved = False
        best_fr = 0.0
        best_trial = M

        for fr in frac_primary:
            delta = fr * pd
            if delta <= 1e-15:
                continue
            Mtr = evaluate_exchange_delta(c, diff, j, d, temp, delta)
            if Mtr < best_trial - 5e-18:
                best_trial = Mtr
                best_fr = fr
            if Mtr < M - 5e-18:
                improved = True

        if not improved:
            for fr in frac_secondary:
                delta = fr * pd
                if delta <= 1e-15:
                    continue
                Mtr = evaluate_exchange_delta(c, diff, j, d, temp, delta)
                if Mtr < best_trial - 5e-18:
                    best_trial = Mtr
                    best_fr = fr
                if Mtr < M - 5e-18:
                    improved = True

        if improved and best_fr > 0.0:
            delta = best_fr * pd
            d2 = delta * delta
            two_j = 2 * j
            two_d = 2 * d
            jd = j + d

            c += (2.0 * delta) * diff
            c[two_j] += d2
            c[two_d] += d2
            c[jd] -= 2.0 * d2

            p[j] += delta
            p[d] -= delta
            if p[d] < 0.0:
                p[d] = 0.0

            ss = float(p.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ss) or abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                p = normalize_vec(p)
                c = convolve_norm(p)
            else:
                # Very light normalization to keep accumulations bounded.
                if abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-12:
                    p /= ss
                    c /= (ss * ss)

            if steps % 40 == 0:
                if float(c.min()) < -1e-9:
                    p = normalize_vec(p)
                    c = convolve_norm(p)

            M = float(c.max())
            steps += 1
            stall = 0

            if M < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m = M
                best = p.copy()
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                p0 = p.copy()
                c0 = c.copy()
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.04 * min(3, stall // 50)
                pp = perturb_sequence(p, rng, sigma)
                cc = convolve_norm(pp)
                MM = float(cc.max())
                if np.isfinite(MM) and MM <= max(best_m * 1.25, best_m + 1e-7):
                    p = pp
                    c = cc
                    M = MM
                else:
                    p = p0
                    c = c0
                    M = best_m
                stall = 0

    return best, float(best_m)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # First: try the provided external construction, if present.
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if 8 <= par.size <= 20000:
            p = normalize_vec(par)
            c = convolve_norm(p)
            M = float(c.max())
            sc = 2.0 * p.size * M
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                print(f"parent sequence length={p.size} score={sc:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Allocate time among several lengths and several initial shapes.
    n_budget = {
        6121: 780,
        8192: 780,
        4096: 300,
        2048: 200,
        1024: 160,
    }
    n_order = [6121, 8192, 4096, 2048, 1024]

    for n in n_order:
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if remaining < 70.0:
            break
        allot = min(float(n_budget.get(n, 120)), remaining - 5.0)
        if allot < 50.0:
            continue

        raw = make_initials(n, rng)
        topk = 3 if n >= 4096 else 4
        top = score_initials(n, raw, topk)
        if not top:
            continue

        # Spend most of this length's budget on the best starting point.
        t = allot
        for i, (M0, p0, c0) in enumerate(top):
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 30.0:
                break
            if i == 0:
                lim = min(t * 0.57, remaining - 5.0)
            elif i == 1:
                lim = min(t * 0.30, remaining - 5.0)
            else:
                lim = min(t * 0.13, remaining - 5.0)
            if lim < 15.0:
                continue
            t -= lim
            sc0 = 2.0 * n * M0
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < best_score:
                best_score = sc0
                best_p = p0.copy()
                print(f"n={n} init[{i}] score={sc0:.6f}")

            bp, bm = optimize_exchange(n, p0, c0, M0, lim, rng)
            sc = 2.0 * n * bm
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = bp.copy()
                print(f"n={n} opt[{i}] score={sc:.6f} M={bm:.8f}")

    # Final polish on the current global best.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_p is not None and remaining > 120.0:
        n = best_p.size
        p = best_p.copy()
        c = convolve_norm(p)
        M = float(c.max())
        lim = min(remaining - 6.0, 700.0)
        if lim > 20.0:
            bp, bm = optimize_exchange(n, p, c, M, lim, rng)
            sc = 2.0 * n * bm
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = bp.copy()
                print(f"final polish score={sc:.6f}")

    # If we somehow have no valid candidate, use a solid deterministic fallback.
    if best_p is None or (not np.all(np.isfinite(best_p))) or best_p.size < 1:
        n = 1024
        a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0)
        best_p = normalize_vec(a)

    # Final exact validation. If it fails, fall back to a simple normalized sequence.
    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            best_score = exact
            print(f"final exact score={best_score:.6f} length={best_p.size}")
        else:
            raise ValueError("invalid final sequence")
    except Exception:
        n = 1024
        a = 1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0)
        best_p = normalize_vec(a)
        try:
            exact = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_p]))
            if np.isfinite(exact):
                best_score = exact
        except Exception:
            best_score = 2.0

    return [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_p, 0.0, 1000.0)]
```
